### S53 executable-specification authoring evidence

The following is source authoring evidence, not executed validation or
independent review. It records all existing scenarios in the 20 owned suites,
including the two explicitly preserved historical Hygiene scenarios. Multiple
When/Then entries are existing sequential behavior within the same case.
Each line number identifies the actual setup, action, expectation or should
matcher in the authored Scala file. No new behavior case was introduced.

| Existing scenario and disposition | Given setup | When action | Then expectation | Actual should vocabulary |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `SbtReviewCiGateSpec.scala` L15: preserve each CBD-owned pass, fail, and unknown result with its contract exit code (existing affected behavior) | L16: `Given("three accepted artifact manifests")` | L19: `When("the CI gate reads each manifest")` | L24: `Then("it uses the manifest result and never derives a local conclusion")` | L25: `results shouldBe cases.map { case (result, exitcode) => Right(SbtReviewCiGate(result, exitcode)) }` |
| `SbtReviewCiGateSpec.scala` L28: refuse a manifest whose exit code disagrees with the CBD-owned gate (existing affected behavior) | L29: `Given("one failing gate with a substituted passing exit code")` | L31: `When("the CI consumer validates the accepted manifest")` | L34: `Then("the task cannot reinterpret the Review result")` | L35: `result shouldBe Left("cbd-review-ci-manifest-exit-code-mismatch")` |
| `SbtReviewCiGateSpec.scala` L38: refuse incomplete or path-substituted manifests even when their gate and exit code agree (existing affected behavior) | L39: `Given("one incomplete manifest and one complete manifest with a substituted artifact path")` | L48: `When("the CI consumer validates its artifact-manifest boundary")` | L52: `Then("neither document is accepted as a CI gate input")` | L53: `incompleteresult shouldBe Left("cbd-review-ci-manifest-shape-invalid")` |

## S54 repair20 R3 SQL JSON presence repair — 2026-10-10

`P715-02-S54-SQL-NULL-FIX20` addresses
`P715-S54-SQL-EXPLICIT-NULL-LOSS-001` and is
**implemented-unvalidated-unreviewed**. The SQL object decoder's
`RecordDecoder` / `Record.create` path and recursive `Record.dataAuto`
conversion discarded explicit JSON null, empty objects and empty arrays.
The result-row `Record.create` also discarded a decoded root empty object.
These losses altered persisted canonical metadata and therefore the existing
semantic reuse identity / issued Owner comparison.

The frozen R3 repair changes only:

- `/Users/asami/src/dev2025/cloud-native-component-framework/src/main/scala/org/goldenport/cncf/datastore/sql/SqlDataStore.scala`
- `/Users/asami/src/dev2025/cloud-native-component-framework/src/test/scala/org/goldenport/cncf/datastore/SqliteDataStoreSpec.scala`

The decoder now constructs complete JSON objects with `Record.createFull`,
retaining null and empty structures recursively. The result row uses
`Record.createFull` after filtering actual JDBC SQL NULL values, preserving
SQL NULL omission while retaining a present JSON `{}`. The old numeric modes
remain separate: root-object numbers and their descendants use `BigDecimal`;
root-array numbers and their descendants use `Long` or `Double`. Plain,
whitespace and malformed object/array text retain their original fallback.
The two existing value-object round-trip scenarios include the frozen object
and array fixtures, precise decimal / nested presence expectations and
`Update.SetNull` input for the SQL NULL distinction. Canonical explicit-null
reuse metadata, identity and issued Owner admission remain unchanged.

The prior R2 worker returned pre-edit `SCOPE_MISMATCH` without changing owned
files. The parent re-planned R3 to include the same-file result-row constructor
and preserve both numeric modes, then assigned this fresh worker. The first
R3 scoped write was admitted but its Perl edit payload aborted before writes
with exit 255 because of `@version` interpolation; the corrected scoped write
completed with exit 0 for both paths. No permission denial occurred.

`SqliteDataStoreSpec`, `EntityStoreRecordProjectionSpec` and
`SqliteConditionalTransitionSpec`; exact normal
`org.goldenport:goldenport-cncf_3:0.5.3-SNAPSHOT` refresh through `publishLocal`;
then the original 41 S54 suites (36 Textus, four sbt-cozy and one Cozy).

remaining 34 were not run. This edit supplies no passing validation or
review evidence. Source-bound E01 / Bloop output admission remains OPEN.

and distinct release remain OPEN. Repository-full and broader integration
